- **manage-npm-modules**: Install, list, inspect, or uninstall npm modules for Neptune DXP server scripts via the MCP tools `list_npm_modules`, `get_npm_module`, `install_npm_module`, `uninstall_npm_module`. ([neptune-software/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/neptune-software/claude-code-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/manage-npm-modules) · ★2)
